        "key": "10-work-shapes",
        "title": "The three missing work shapes and genome profiles",
        "spec": "# Implementation Spec — The Three Missing Work Shapes, and the Genome Profile That Makes One Engine Fit Them All\n\n**Basis:** `C:/kfm`, branch `main` @ `71a6fb0b`, read 2026-08-09. Builds on `docs/CAPABILITY_MAP_2026-08-09.md` and `docs/OPEN_GAPS_2026-08-09.md`; does not repeat them.\n\n**Numbers re-derived, not quoted:** `grep -c '^model ' packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma` → **439**. `ls apps/server/src/modules | wc -l` → **110**. `find apps/web/src/app/app -name page.tsx | wc -l` → **209**. Unique `name: '…'` in `flow-tool-registry.ts` → **245**. `@Cron(` → **26**, `setInterval(` → **52**, `@Interval(` → **4**, `forwardRef(` → **189 occurrences**. `bullmq: ^5.76.8` is at `apps/server/package.json:42`.\n\n---\n\n## 0. What exists today. Reuse before you add.\n\nI opened every file below.\n\n### 0.1 The three shapes that exist\n\n| Shape | Model | Line | Module |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| appointment | `Booking` | `schema.prisma:3776` | `modules/bookings` |\n| cohort | `Cohort` / `CourseEnrollment` | `:5323` / `:5303` | `modules/community`, `modules/education` |\n| project | `Project` / `ProjectTask` | `:4221` / `:4266` | `modules/projects` |\n\n### 0.2 The three that do not — verified absent\n\n```\ngrep -oP \"^model \\K\\w+\" packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma \\\n  | grep -iE \"shift|roster|work|job|case|polic|claim|visit|dispatch|technician|unit|equip|maint|benefic\"\n```\nReturns `JobRole`, `Workflow*`, `CrossModuleWorkflow`, `Community*`, `ContactExportJob`, `SyncJob`, `ScheduledAgentJob`, `ComplianceGuidanceProfile` — and **nothing** named WorkOrder, Site-of-service, ServicedUnit, Case, Policy, Shift, Roster, Attendance or Beneficiary. `Site` (`:4501`) is a *website* (`subdomain`, `siteData`). `Asset` (`:10468`) is a media file. `FixedAsset` (`:10100`) is a depreciation schedule. None of the three is a customer's serviced equipment.\n\n### 0.3 The eight things I am reusing rather than building\n\n| Asset | Where | What it gives me |\n|---|---|---|\n| **`CommandItem`** | `schema.prisma:7931`, unique key at **`:7971`** `@@unique([businessId, sourceModule, sourceType, sourceId, actionType])` | **The obligation ledger already exists.** It has `dueAt`, `snoozedUntil`, `riskTier`, `executableByKey`, `executionTool`, `executionPayload`, `entityType/entityId`. |\n| **Its manual screen** | `apps/web/src/app/app/command-center/`, API at `apps/web/src/lib/api/command.ts:47-145`, controller `modules/command/command.controller.ts:21-174` | complete / dismiss / snooze / approve / execute / assign / reopen are **already** human-operable. My three modules inherit a finished manual surface for the obligation itself. |\n| **`OrgAssignment`** | `schema.prisma:2296` | The technician and the security guard, already modelled: `isContactOnly`, `contactPhone` (E.164, normalised), `preferredChannel: 'whatsapp'`, `autoApprovalViaReply`, `jobRoleId`, `orgUnitId`, `reportsToId`. A guard who will never log in is exactly this row. Do not invent an `Employee`. |\n| **`StaffMember` + `Availability` + `Skill`** | `:3689`, `:3747` | Weekly availability windows and a skill taxonomy for roster matching. |\n| **BullMQ, wired** | `modules/ai/queue.service.ts:113-132` (repeatable upsert with `jobId` + `repeat:{pattern,tz}`), `modules/business-events/business-event.queue.ts:36-58` (house Queue/Worker shape), `modules/temporal-flow/temporal-flow-memory.queue.service.ts:32-38` (ping-then-degrade) | Every clock in this spec. No new dependency, no new container. |\n| **Cortex tool bridge** | `flow-tool-registry.ts:4919` `CORTEX_TOOL_BRIDGE`, dispatch at `flow-orchestrator.service.ts:5307-5332` via `this.moduleRef.get(KeyCortexToolRegistryService, { strict: false })`; registry `register`/`registerMany` at `key-cortex-tool-registry.service.ts:142,182` | **The zero-edge path to give KEY a tool.** See §1.3. |\n| **`BusinessBlueprint.workflowModel`** | `schema.prisma:9025`, inferred at `modules/blueprint/blueprint.service.ts:1179-1192`, read at **`:649-654` only**, where it becomes the prose `Primary workflow: X.` | Half a genome profile already exists, written and never read for behaviour. §2 promotes it instead of adding a rival. |\n| **`ScheduledAgentJob`** | `schema.prisma:4163`, `@@unique([businessId, entityId, checkpoint])` | Precedent for a durable, idempotent, tenant-scoped scheduled row. I use BullMQ instead (§1.4) and say why. |\n\n### 0.4 What I am *not* reusing, and why\n\n- **`CommandBridgeService.bridge()`** (`modules/intelligence/command-bridge.service.ts:31-46`) does `findFirst` then `create` — a check-then-act race that ignores the unique index one table away. Two workers raising the same obligation in the same second produce a `P2002` that the caller does not handle. I write a new listener that uses `upsert` on the real key (§1.2). Existing callers are untouched.\n- **`Business.metaData`** (`schema.prisma:608`) and the pattern at `modules/projects/project-revenue.listener.ts:60-80` (config read out of a JSON blob). Unqueryable, unvalidated, invisible to the tenant extension. The genome profile gets its own table.\n- **`ResilientEmitterService`** (`modules/business-events/resilient-emitter.service.ts:16-20`) keeps `state`, `failures`, `halfOpenCalls` in **module fields**. That is a per-replica circuit breaker: with two replicas the breaker opens twice and half-opens twice. It is the exact anti-pattern the SCALABLE constraint names. Nothing in this spec holds state in a field.\n\n---\n\n## 1. The shared contract: what \"emits an obligation\" means\n\nAll three modules do one thing in common and nothing else in common. That one thing is defined once, here, and it is **an event, not a service call**.\n\n### 1.1 The event pair\n\nNew file: `apps/server/src/core/event-bus/obligation.types.ts` (beside `events.types.ts`, which already holds `BookingCreatedPayload` etc.).\n\n```ts\nexport type WorkShape =\n  | 'appointment' | 'cohort' | 'dispatch' | 'project' | 'compliance' | 'case' | 'shift';\n\n/** Raised when a work record acquires a deadline someone must meet. */\nexport class ObligationRaisedPayload {\n  businessId!: string;\n  shape!: WorkShape;\n  /** Identity of the obligation. These five are the CommandItem unique key. */\n  sourceModule!: 'dispatch' | 'casework' | 'roster';\n  sourceType!: string;          // 'work_order' | 'case_requirement' | 'shift' | 'case_policy'\n  sourceId!: string;\n  actionType!: string;          // 'SLA_BREACH_RISK' | 'RENEWAL_DUE' | 'SHIFT_UNFILLED' | …\n  /** Payload. */\n  title!: string;\n  description?: string;\n  category!: 'WORK' | 'PEOPLE' | 'GOVERNANCE' | 'MONEY';\n  dueAt!: Date;\n  priority!: number;            // 0..100\n  urgency!: number;             // 0..100\n  riskTier!: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;\n  contactId?: string;\n  entityType!: string;\n  entityId!: string;\n  /** Set only when a real, registered KEY tool can discharge it. */\n  executionTool?: string;\n  executionPayload?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\n/** Raised when the obligation is met, cancelled, or has become moot. */\nexport class ObligationSettledPayload {\n  businessId!: string;\n  sourceModule!: 'dispatch' | 'casework' | 'roster';\n  sourceType!: string;\n  sourceId!: string;\n  actionType!: string;\n  outcome!: 'MET' | 'CANCELLED' | 'SUPERSEDED';\n}\n```\n\nEvent names: `work.obligation.raised`, `work.obligation.settled`. Emitted through the **global** `EventEmitter2` (`EventBusModule` is `@Global` — `core/event-bus/event-bus.module.ts:4`), so emitting requires no module import in either direction.\n\n`executionTool` may only name a tool that exists in `FLOW_TOOLS`. That is asserted by a spec (§8.6), because `AutopilotTask.autoExecutable` is written at seven sites and read by nothing, and `CommandItem.executionTool` is one string away from becoming the same defect.\n\n### 1.2 The single consumer\n\nNew file: `apps/server/src/modules/command/obligation.listener.ts`. It lives in `command` because `command` owns `CommandItem`, and **no other module may write that table** — that is the modular rule applied, not asserted.\n\n```ts\n@Injectable()\nexport class ObligationListener {\n  constructor(@Inject(PrismaService) private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}\n\n  @OnEvent('work.obligation.raised', { async: true })\n  async onRaised(p: ObligationRaisedPayload) {\n    // The unique index at schema.prisma:7971 IS the idempotency key. upsert,\n    // not findFirst-then-create: two workers can raise the same obligation in\n    // the same millisecond and exactly one row must result.\n    await this.prisma.client.commandItem.upsert({\n      where: { businessId_sourceModule_sourceType_sourceId_actionType: {\n        businessId: p.businessId, sourceModule: p.sourceModule,\n        sourceType: p.sourceType, sourceId: p.sourceId, actionType: p.actionType } },\n      create: { …, status: 'OPEN', recommendedBy: 'SYSTEM' },\n      update: { dueAt: p.dueAt, priority: p.priority, urgency: p.urgency,\n                title: p.title, description: p.description },\n      // NOT status: re-raising must never reopen an item a human dismissed.\n    });\n  }\n\n  @OnEvent('work.obligation.settled', { async: true })\n  async onSettled(p: ObligationSettledPayload) { /* updateMany → COMPLETED/DISMISSED, guarded on status */ }\n}\n```\n\nRegistered in `modules/command/command.module.ts` `providers`. That module currently imports `PrismaModule, AuthModule, TimelineModule, NotificationsModule` — **unchanged**. The listener injects `PrismaService` only.\n\n`command.module.ts` gains a provider. It gains no import. That is the whole edit to an existing module on the server side, apart from the registry entries in §6.\n\n### 1.3 Why none of this adds an edge to the `forwardRef` graph\n\nThe measured problem is 189 `forwardRef(` occurrences and four surviving cycles. A Nest cycle needs a path *back*. Three rules make one impossible here:\n\n1. **The three new modules import nothing but `WorkProfileModule`.** `PrismaModule` (`core/prisma/prisma.module.ts:4`), `RedisModule` (`core/redis/redis.module.ts:53`) and `EventBusModule` are all `@Global`; a module that needs Prisma, Redis and the event bus declares **`imports: []`**.\n2. **Nothing existing imports the three new modules.** Not `AiModule`, not `KeyCortexModule`, not `CommandModule`. Everything they need to say leaves as an event.\n3. **KEY reaches their tools without an import.** `flow-orchestrator.service.ts` is 5,885 lines and imports ~85 services directly (lines 5-89); adding a 86th is how the cycle count grows. Instead each new module implements `OnModuleInit` and does:\n\n```ts\n// dispatch/dispatch-tool.registrar.ts\ntry {\n  const registry = this.moduleRef.get(KeyCortexToolRegistryService, { strict: false });\n  registry.registerMany(DISPATCH_CORTEX_TOOLS.map(t => ({ ...t, handler: … })));\n} catch { this.logger.warn('cortex registry not loaded; dispatch tools unavailable to KEY'); }\n```\n\n`strict: false` searches the whole application container regardless of module exports — this is precisely why `flow-orchestrator.service.ts:5316` uses it. The direction of the *file* import is `dispatch → key-cortex`, one way, and `key-cortex` never imports `dispatch`. Then `CORTEX_TOOL_BRIDGE` in `flow-tool-registry.ts:4919` gains twelve entries mapping `dispatch_*` → `dispatch.*`, and `flow-orchestrator.service.ts` gains **zero new lines** — the `default:` branch at `:5280` already dispatches every bridged name.\n\nI did *not* extend `KeyCortexOrganRegistrarService` (`key-cortex-organ-registrar.service.ts:26-32`), which injects six adapters behind `forwardRef` and would need a seventh. Self-registration from the owning module inverts that dependency.\n\n> **The check that this held:** `apps/server/test/app-module-boots.integration.test.ts` already exists and boots the real container. If a cycle were introduced, it fails in about five seconds — the fast falsifier `OPEN_GAPS` §\"Decisions, not defects\" describes.\n\n### 1.4 The clock: BullMQ, never `setInterval`\n\nEvery timer in this spec is a BullMQ job. There are 52 `setInterval` schedulers and zero distributed locks today; a second replica double-fires all of them. BullMQ repeatable jobs are deduplicated *by construction*: `queue.add(name, data, { jobId, repeat })` with the same `jobId` from N replicas produces one schedule. That is leader election with no leader.\n\nEach module owns one queue and one worker:\n\n| Queue | Repeatable jobs (deterministic `jobId`) | Per-entity delayed jobs |\n|---|---|---|\n| `dispatch-clock` | `dispatch:sla-sweep` (`*/5 * * * *`), `dispatch:service-due-sweep` (`0 6 * * *`) | `dispatch:sla:<workOrderId>:<dueAtMs>` |\n| `casework-clock` | `casework:renewal-sweep` (`0 7 * * *`), `casework:requirement-sweep` (`0 7 * * *`) | `casework:renewal:<policyId>:<noticeAtMs>` |\n| `roster-clock` | `roster:materialise` (`0 2 * * *`), `roster:unfilled-sweep` (`*/15 * * * *`), `roster:no-show-sweep` (`*/5 * * * *`) | `roster:noshow:<shiftId>:<graceEndMs>` |\n\nRules, non-negotiable:\n\n- **`onModuleInit` pings Redis first and returns on failure**, copying `temporal-flow-memory.queue.service.ts:32-38`. `redis.module.ts:118` sets `enableOfflineQueue: false` after a measured incident where a command against a dead Redis never settled and the whole boot hung. A worker that awaits Redis without a guard reintroduces that.\n- **Repeatable upsert is remove-then-add**, copying `queue.service.ts:116-131`, so a changed cron pattern does not leave an orphan schedule.\n- **Delayed jobs carry the target time in the `jobId`.** Rescheduling a work order removes the old id and adds the new one; the identity of a job is (entity, deadline), so a stale timer cannot fire against a moved deadline.\n- **`attempts: 3, backoff: exponential`, `removeOnComplete: { age: 86400, count: 1000 }`.**\n- **No worker holds state in a field.** Cursors live in the row (`WorkOrder.slaEscalatedAt`, `Shift.noShowRaisedAt`), so restart and replica count change nothing.\n- **Every query inside a worker carries an explicit `where: { businessId }`.** `packages/db/src/client.ts:121-124` states it plainly: the tenant interceptor is HTTP-only, so `activeBusinessId()` is `undefined` on cron/BullMQ/webhook paths and the isolation extension does nothing there. The membership of `BUSINESS_ID_MODELS` is the second layer for HTTP; the explicit `where` is the only layer for a worker.\n\nWhy not `ScheduledAgentJob` (`schema.prisma:4163`), which already has the right unique key? Because it needs a poller to drive it, and the only poller shape available is `@Interval` — the thing this constraint forbids. BullMQ *is* the durable timer, and `flow-runner.service.ts:283` already contains the comment asking for it.\n\n---\n\n## 2. The genome profile: vocabulary and rules, no code change\n\n### 2.1 What is broken\n\n`BusinessBlueprint.workflowModel` (`schema.prisma:9025`) already asks the right question. `blueprint.service.ts:1179-1192` infers `{ appointmentBooking, projectManagement, retainerCycle, walkInQueue, ecommerceFulfillment, quoteDrivenSales, customInquiryFlow }` from row counts. Its only reader is `blueprint.service.ts:649-654`, which turns `primaryWorkflow` into the English sentence `Primary workflow: X.` inside a strategy document. **It changes no screen, no nav entry, no tool, no rule.** Written and never read — the third defect class in the capability map, §1.5.\n\nVocabulary does not exist at all. The nearest thing is `KeyCortexExpertiseLensService.matchJobRoleLabel` (`key-cortex-expertise-lens.service.ts:230-270`), which relabels one lens from a business's `JobRole.name`. That is a label on a persona, not a noun for a customer.\n\n### 2.2 The module\n\n**New:** `apps/server/src/modules/business-genome/work-profile/` — physically inside `business-genome`, so the genome owns it as the owner asked, but its own Nest module.\n\n```ts\n// work-profile.module.ts\n@Module({\n  imports: [PrismaModule],\n  controllers: [WorkProfileController],\n  providers: [WorkProfileService],\n  exports: [WorkProfileService],\n})\nexport class WorkProfileModule {}\n```\n\n**It must not be folded into `BusinessGenomeModule`.** I traced that graph: `BusinessGenomeModule` (`business-genome.module.ts:14`) imports `BlueprintModule`, which imports `TemporalFlowModule` (`blueprint.module.ts:9`), which imports **`forwardRef(() => AiModule)`** (`temporal-flow.module.ts:15`). Any module that imports `BusinessGenomeModule` is pulled into the `AiModule` cycle cluster. `WorkProfileModule` is modelled on `KeyGenomeModule` instead (`key-genome/key-genome.module.ts:36`: `imports: [PrismaModule]`), which is already a clean leaf. `BusinessGenomeModule` may import `WorkProfileModule` later; the reverse is forbidden and is asserted in §8.1.\n\n**Public interface — the whole of it:**\n\n```ts\nexport interface ResolvedWorkProfile {\n  businessId: string;\n  packKey: WorkProfilePackKey;\n  version: number;\n  enabledShapes: WorkShape[];\n  vocabulary: Record<WorkTermKey, { one: string; many: string }>;\n  rules: WorkRules;              // typed, closed set — see 2.4\n  resolvedAt: Date;\n}\n\nclass WorkProfileService {\n  resolve(businessId: string): Promise<ResolvedWorkProfile>;       // pack ⊕ overrides, cached in Redis 5 min\n  isShapeEnabled(businessId: string, shape: WorkShape): Promise<boolean>;\n  term(profile: ResolvedWorkProfile, key: WorkTermKey, plural?: boolean): string;  // pure\n  rule<K extends keyof WorkRules>(profile: ResolvedWorkProfile, key: K): WorkRules[K];  // pure\n  glossaryBlock(profile: ResolvedWorkProfile): string;             // pure — for the KEY prompt\n}\n```\n\nListens to: nothing. Emits: `work.profile.changed { businessId, packKey, version }` on write, so caches invalidate. (Emitting an event nothing listens to is fine; a *listener* with no emitter is what `event-wiring.spec.ts:88-97` forbids.)\n\n### 2.3 Vocabulary — a closed term set, presentation only\n\n```ts\nexport type WorkTermKey =\n  | 'client'      // client | patient | student | constituent | beneficiary | member | guest\n  | 'worker'      // technician | guard | tutor | case officer | crew\n  | 'job'         // work order | visit | case | shift | session | gig\n  | 'site'        // site | premises | branch | post | venue\n  | 'unit'        // unit | equipment | asset | vehicle\n  | 'engagement'  // policy | enrolment | grant | retainer | contract\n  | 'obligation'; // task | requirement | renewal | callout\n```\n\nThe packs are **checked-in TypeScript**, not database rows:\n\n```ts\n// work-profile/work-profile.packs.ts\nexport const WORK_PROFILE_PACKS: Record<WorkProfilePackKey, WorkProfilePack> = {\n  field_service: {                       // the AC company\n    enabledShapes: ['dispatch', 'appointment', 'compliance'],\n    vocabulary: { client: ['customer','customers'], worker: ['technician','technicians'],\n                  job: ['work order','work orders'], site: ['site','sites'],\n                  unit: ['unit','units'], engagement: ['service contract','service contracts'],\n                  obligation: ['callout','callouts'] },\n    rules: { 'dispatch.slaHoursByPriority': { P1: 4, P2: 24, P3: 72 },\n             'dispatch.requireSignatureOnCompletion': true,\n             'dispatch.serviceIntervalMonthsDefault': 6 },\n  },\n  brokerage: {                           // the insurance broker\n    enabledShapes: ['case', 'compliance'],\n    vocabulary: { client: ['client','clients'], worker: ['account manager','account managers'],\n                  job: ['case','cases'], engagement: ['policy','policies'],\n                  obligation: ['renewal','renewals'], site: ['address','addresses'], unit: ['risk','risks'] },\n    rules: { 'casework.renewalNoticeDays': 45, 'casework.escalateAtDays': 14 },\n  },\n  ngo_casework: {                        // the NGO / the ministry\n    enabledShapes: ['case', 'cohort', 'compliance'],\n    vocabulary: { client: ['beneficiary','beneficiaries'], worker: ['case officer','case officers'],\n                  job: ['case','cases'], engagement: ['grant','grants'],\n                  obligation: ['requirement','requirements'], site: ['community','communities'], unit: ['household','households'] },\n    rules: { 'casework.renewalNoticeDays': 90, 'casework.requireEvidenceOnClose': true },\n  },\n  guarding: { enabledShapes: ['shift','dispatch'], … },     // the security firm\n  academy:  { enabledShapes: ['cohort','appointment'], … }, // the music academy — 'student'\n  clinic:   { enabledShapes: ['appointment','case'], … },   // 'patient'\n  ministry: { enabledShapes: ['case','project','compliance'], … }, // 'constituent'\n  solo_gig: { enabledShapes: ['appointment','project'], … }, // the DJ — 'client', job = 'gig'\n  agency:   { enabledShapes: ['project','compliance'], … },\n  salon:    { enabledShapes: ['appointment'], … },\n};\n```\n\n**Three constraints on vocabulary, each load-bearing:**\n\n1. **It never reaches the database.** Columns stay canonical English (`contactId`, `WorkOrder`, `assignmentId`). Per-tenant column naming is a schema fork wearing a config hat, and it would break `BUSINESS_ID_MODELS`, which is matched by model name as a string (`packages/db/src/client.ts:198`).\n2. **It never reaches a tool name.** `FLOW_TOOLS` stays one canonical set of 245+N names. `check-tool-routes` matches a tool's domain prefix against its route by substring (`tool-route-audit.ts:117-121`); per-tenant names would make that check meaningless. What KEY gets instead is a **glossary block** prepended to the system prompt: `In this business a customer is called a \"beneficiary\" and a job is called a \"case\". Use these words; the tools keep their own names.`\n3. **The term set is closed.** `WorkTermKey` is a union, so a pack that invents `'patron'` fails typecheck rather than rendering `undefined` in a heading.\n\n### 2.4 Rules — a closed typed set, deliberately not a DSL\n\n```ts\nexport interface WorkRules {\n  'dispatch.slaHoursByPriority': { P1: number; P2: number; P3: number };\n  'dispatch.requireSignatureOnCompletion': boolean;\n  'dispatch.serviceIntervalMonthsDefault': number;\n  'dispatch.autoInvoiceOnCompletion': boolean;\n  'casework.renewalNoticeDays': number;\n  'casework.escalateAtDays': number;\n  'casework.requireEvidenceOnClose': boolean;\n  'roster.materialiseHorizonDays': number;\n  'roster.unfilledAlertHoursBefore': number;\n  'roster.lateGraceMinutes': number;\n  'roster.requireGeoOnClockIn': boolean;\n}\n```\n\nEvery key has a type, a default in `WORK_PROFILE_DEFAULTS`, and a validator. A rule the engine does not read cannot be declared, because the interface is the list of what is read.\n\nThis is the single most important refusal in the document. The SMART layer already advertises 32 triggers and 25 actions against an engine implementing 8 and 6, and logs `outcome: 'success'` either way (`capability map` §1.3). A genome rule *language* would be that failure again, one level more expressive. **A rule is a typed value the engine consumes at a named line, or it does not exist.**\n\n### 2.5 Data\n\n```prisma\nmodel WorkProfile {\n  id            String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId    String   @unique @map(\"business_id\")\n  business      Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  packKey       String   @map(\"pack_key\")           // field_service | brokerage | ngo_casework | …\n  schemaVersion Int      @default(1) @map(\"schema_version\")\n\n  /// Overrides ONLY. The pack in work-profile.packs.ts is the base; this holds\n  /// what one business changed. Never the whole map — a full copy silently\n  /// stops tracking pack improvements, which is how Business.metaData rotted.\n  vocabularyOverrides Json @default(\"{}\") @map(\"vocabulary_overrides\")\n  ruleOverrides       Json @default(\"{}\") @map(\"rule_overrides\")\n\n  /// Explicit wins over inference. Null means \"use the pack's enabledShapes\".\n  enabledShapes String[] @default([]) @map(\"enabled_shapes\")\n\n  /// PACK | OWNER | INFERRED — provenance, so an owner's choice is never\n  /// overwritten by the next inference pass.\n  source        String   @default(\"PACK\")\n  confirmedAt   DateTime? @map(\"confirmed_at\")\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId])\n  @@index([businessId, packKey])\n  @@map(\"work_profiles\")\n}\n```\n\n`packKey` is seeded from `Business.archetype` (`schema.prisma:224`) and `BusinessBlueprint.workflowModel` at onboarding, then confirmed by the owner on `/app/settings/work-profile`. `blueprint.service.ts:1192` keeps inferring; when it disagrees with a row whose `source = 'OWNER'`, it raises an obligation (`actionType: 'WORK_PROFILE_DRIFT'`) rather than overwriting. That is the \"operators edit upstream, the AI reconciles\" rule the blueprint model already states at `schema.prisma:9016-9018`, actually enforced.\n\n### 2.6 The web side\n\n- `apps/web/src/lib/api/work-profile.ts` — `fetchWorkProfile`, `updateWorkProfile`.\n- `apps/web/src/contexts/work-profile-context.tsx` — modelled on `contexts/genome-context.tsx:27-60` (same `getStoredBusinessId()` + reset-on-business-change shape). Exposes `useTerm()`.\n- `NavItem` in `apps/web/src/lib/nav-config.ts:70-77` gains `workShape?: WorkShape`. `apps/web/src/hooks/use-app-layout.ts` filters on the resolved profile, next to the existing `dormantFlag` and disclosure-mode label filters. The nav *entries stay in the file always*, so `nav-destinations.spec.ts` (static) and `nav-reachability.spec.ts` (static) keep passing — visibility is runtime, reachability is source.\n- Every heading, column and empty state in the three new screens reads its noun from `useTerm()`. The negative control in §8.5 is what stops that being decoration.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Module A — `dispatch` (job/dispatch: the AC company)\n\n`apps/server/src/modules/dispatch/`\n\n### 3.1 Boundary\n\n**Owns:** `ServiceSite`, `ServicedUnit`, `WorkOrder`, `WorkOrderLine`, `WorkOrderVisit`.\n**Public interface:** `DispatchService` (exported, currently consumed by nobody), the HTTP controller, and twelve cortex tools.\n**Listens to:** `booking.completed` (an appointment that revealed a fault can spawn a work order — subscribed only when `rules['dispatch.autoWorkOrderFromBooking']`), `invoice.paid` (settles the `AWAITING_PAYMENT` obligation).\n**Emits:** `work.obligation.raised` / `work.obligation.settled`, plus `dispatch.work_order.created|assigned|completed|cancelled`, `dispatch.unit.service_due`.\n**Imports:** `[WorkProfileModule]`. Nothing else.\n**Imported by:** nothing.\n\nWhy no cycle: `dispatch → work-profile → (globals)`. `work-profile` imports `PrismaModule` only. There is no path from any existing module into `dispatch`, so no back-edge can exist.\n\n### 3.2 Data\n\n```prisma\nmodel ServiceSite {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  contactId  String   @map(\"contact_id\")\n  contact    Contact  @relation(fields: [contactId], references: [id], onDelete: NoAction)\n\n  name       String\n  address    String?\n  city       String?\n  country    String?  @default(\"TT\")\n  placeId    String?  @map(\"place_id\")\n  latLng     Json?    @map(\"lat_lng\")\n  accessNotes String? @map(\"access_notes\") @db.Text\n  orgUnitId  String?  @map(\"org_unit_id\")\n  orgUnit    OrgUnit? @relation(fields: [orgUnitId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n\n  units      ServicedUnit[]\n  workOrders WorkOrder[]\n\n  createdAt DateTime  @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime  @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n  deletedAt DateTime? @map(\"deleted_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId])\n  @@index([businessId, contactId])\n  @@map(\"service_sites\")\n}\n\nmodel ServicedUnit {\n  id         String      @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String      @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business    @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  siteId     String      @map(\"site_id\")\n  site       ServiceSite @relation(fields: [siteId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  label            String                  // \"Server room split, 2nd floor\"\n  category         String                  // hvac | generator | pump | vehicle | other\n  manufacturer     String?\n  modelNumber      String?  @map(\"model_number\")\n  serialNumber     String?  @map(\"serial_number\")\n  installedAt      DateTime? @map(\"installed_at\")\n  warrantyUntil    DateTime? @map(\"warranty_until\")\n  serviceIntervalMonths Int? @map(\"service_interval_months\")\n  lastServicedAt   DateTime? @map(\"last_serviced_at\")\n  nextServiceDueAt DateTime? @map(\"next_service_due_at\")\n  /// The unit's own history is the join, not a duplicated log.\n  workOrders WorkOrder[]\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@unique([businessId, serialNumber])\n  @@index([businessId, siteId])\n  @@index([businessId, nextServiceDueAt])   // drives dispatch:service-due-sweep\n  @@map(\"serviced_units\")\n}\n\nmodel WorkOrder {\n  id          String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId  String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business    Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  orderNumber String   @map(\"order_number\")\n  contactId   String   @map(\"contact_id\")\n  contact     Contact  @relation(fields: [contactId], references: [id], onDelete: NoAction)\n  siteId      String?  @map(\"site_id\")\n  site        ServiceSite?  @relation(fields: [siteId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n  unitId      String?  @map(\"unit_id\")\n  unit        ServicedUnit? @relation(fields: [unitId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n\n  /// The technician IS an OrgAssignment (schema.prisma:2296) — including the\n  /// contact-only kind reachable over WhatsApp. No new person model.\n  assignmentId String? @map(\"assignment_id\")\n  assignment   OrgAssignment? @relation(fields: [assignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n\n  title       String\n  description String?  @db.Text\n  /// DRAFT | SCHEDULED | DISPATCHED | ON_SITE | AWAITING_PARTS | COMPLETED | CANCELLED\n  status      String   @default(\"DRAFT\")\n  priority    String   @default(\"P3\")          // P1 | P2 | P3\n  source      String   @default(\"MANUAL\")      // MANUAL | KEY | BOOKING | PREVENTIVE | INBOX\n\n  slaDueAt        DateTime? @map(\"sla_due_at\")\n  slaEscalatedAt  DateTime? @map(\"sla_escalated_at\")   // worker cursor — in the row, never a field\n  scheduledStart  DateTime? @map(\"scheduled_start\")\n  scheduledEnd    DateTime? @map(\"scheduled_end\")\n  completedAt     DateTime? @map(\"completed_at\")\n  cancelledAt     DateTime? @map(\"cancelled_at\")\n\n  signatureName String? @map(\"signature_name\")\n  signatureData String? @map(\"signature_data\") @db.Text\n  resolutionNotes String? @map(\"resolution_notes\") @db.Text\n\n  invoiceId String?  @unique @map(\"invoice_id\")\n  invoice   Invoice? @relation(fields: [invoiceId], references: [id], onDelete: NoAction)\n\n  lines  WorkOrderLine[]\n  visits WorkOrderVisit[]\n\n  createdAt DateTime  @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime  @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n  deletedAt DateTime? @map(\"deleted_at\")\n\n  @@unique([businessId, orderNumber])\n  @@index([businessId, status, slaDueAt])       // the SLA sweep's index\n  @@index([businessId, assignmentId, scheduledStart])\n  @@index([businessId, unitId])                 // the unit's service history\n  @@index([businessId, contactId])\n  @@map(\"work_orders\")\n}\n\nmodel WorkOrderLine {\n  id          String    @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId  String    @map(\"business_id\")\n  business    Business  @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  workOrderId String    @map(\"work_order_id\")\n  workOrder   WorkOrder @relation(fields: [workOrderId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  kind        String                       // PART | LABOUR | TRAVEL\n  productId   String?  @map(\"product_id\")\n  product     Product? @relation(fields: [productId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n  description String\n  quantity    Decimal  @default(1) @db.Decimal(12, 3)\n  unitPrice   Decimal  @default(0) @map(\"unit_price\") @db.Decimal(14, 2)\n  currency    String   @default(\"TTD\")\n  billable    Boolean  @default(true)\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId, workOrderId])\n  @@map(\"work_order_lines\")\n}\n\nmodel WorkOrderVisit {\n  id          String    @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId  String    @map(\"business_id\")\n  business    Business  @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  workOrderId String    @map(\"work_order_id\")\n  workOrder   WorkOrder @relation(fields: [workOrderId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  assignmentId String? @map(\"assignment_id\")\n  assignment   OrgAssignment? @relation(fields: [assignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n\n  travelStartedAt DateTime? @map(\"travel_started_at\")\n  arrivedAt       DateTime? @map(\"arrived_at\")\n  departedAt      DateTime? @map(\"departed_at\")\n  travelMinutes   Int?      @map(\"travel_minutes\")\n  onSiteMinutes   Int?      @map(\"on_site_minutes\")\n  odometerStart   Decimal?  @map(\"odometer_start\") @db.Decimal(12, 2)\n  odometerEnd     Decimal?  @map(\"odometer_end\") @db.Decimal(12, 2)\n  arrivalLatLng   Json?     @map(\"arrival_lat_lng\")\n  outcome         String?                       // FIXED | PARTS_NEEDED | NO_ACCESS | REVISIT\n  notes           String?   @db.Text\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId, workOrderId])\n  @@index([businessId, assignmentId, arrivedAt])\n  @@map(\"work_order_visits\")\n}\n```\n\n`OrgAssignment`, `Contact`, `Product`, `Invoice`, `OrgUnit` and `Business` each gain a back-relation field. `Business` gains five. That is the only edit to existing models.\n\n### 3.3 The obligations `dispatch` emits\n\n| `actionType` | Raised by | When | `executionTool` |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `SLA_BREACH_RISK` | `dispatch:sla-sweep` | `slaDueAt` within 25% of the window, `status ∉ (COMPLETED, CANCELLED)`, `slaEscalatedAt IS NULL` | `dispatch_assign_work_order` |\n| `WORK_ORDER_UNASSIGNED` | on create + sweep | `assignmentId IS NULL` and `scheduledStart < now + 24h` | `dispatch_assign_work_order` |\n| `AWAITING_PARTS_STALE` | sweep | `status = 'AWAITING_PARTS'` for > 5 days | — (human) |\n| `SERVICE_DUE` | `dispatch:service-due-sweep` | `ServicedUnit.nextServiceDueAt <= now + 30d` with no open WO | `dispatch_create_work_order` |\n| `COMPLETED_UNBILLED` | sweep | `status = 'COMPLETED'`, `invoiceId IS NULL`, billable lines exist | `dispatch_invoice_work_order` |\n\nSettled by the corresponding state transition inside `DispatchService`, in the same transaction as the write, so a completed job stops nagging in the same request.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Module B — `casework` (case/policy: the broker, the NGO, the ministry)\n\n`apps/server/src/modules/casework/`\n\n### 4.1 Boundary\n\n**Owns:** `CaseFile`, `CasePolicy`, `CaseRequirement`, `CaseEvent`.\n**Listens to:** `contact.created` (only when `rules['casework.autoOpenCaseOnContact']`), `document.processed` (an uploaded document can satisfy a requirement).\n**Emits:** obligations, plus `casework.case.opened|closed`, `casework.policy.renewed|lapsed`.\n**Imports:** `[WorkProfileModule]`. **Imported by:** nothing.\n\n**What it is *not*:** it is not a second `Contract` module. `Contract` (`schema.prisma:857`) already has `renewalDate`, `renewalNoticeDays`, `renewalType`, `expiryDate` and a `ContractAlert` child — and `grep -n '@Cron(' modules/contracts/` returns nothing, which is why renewals have never fired. `CasePolicy` is for the **third-party** obligation a broker administers on a client's behalf (carrier, premium, policy number, the client's renewal, not the business's own agreement). The renewal *engine* is shared: `casework-clock`'s renewal worker reads both `CasePolicy` and `Contract`, which discharges Wave 0 item #2 of the capability map at no extra cost, and it does so by reading a table it does not own **through an event request**, not a query — `casework` emits `compliance.renewal.scan_requested` and the contracts module answers. If that indirection is judged not worth it at build time, the alternative that is still legal is a second repeatable job registered by the *contracts* module on the same queue name. Both are stated so the choice is made deliberately; a cross-module `prisma.client.contract.findMany` from `casework` is not on the list.\n\n### 4.2 Data\n\n```prisma\nmodel CaseFile {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  reference  String   @map(\"reference\")            // human-facing case number\n  contactId  String   @map(\"contact_id\")\n  contact    Contact  @relation(fields: [contactId], references: [id], onDelete: NoAction)\n\n  caseType   String   @map(\"case_type\")            // free per business; the profile names it\n  /// INTAKE | OPEN | AWAITING_CLIENT | AWAITING_THIRD_PARTY | CLOSED | WITHDRAWN\n  status     String   @default(\"INTAKE\")\n  riskLevel  String   @default(\"NORMAL\") @map(\"risk_level\")   // LOW | NORMAL | HIGH | CRITICAL\n\n  assignmentId String? @map(\"assignment_id\")\n  assignment   OrgAssignment? @relation(fields: [assignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n  orgUnitId    String? @map(\"org_unit_id\")\n  orgUnit      OrgUnit? @relation(fields: [orgUnitId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n\n  openedAt   DateTime  @default(now()) @map(\"opened_at\")\n  closedAt   DateTime? @map(\"closed_at\")\n  closeReason String?  @map(\"close_reason\")\n  summary    String?   @db.Text\n\n  policies     CasePolicy[]\n  requirements CaseRequirement[]\n  events       CaseEvent[]\n\n  createdAt DateTime  @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime  @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n  deletedAt DateTime? @map(\"deleted_at\")\n\n  @@unique([businessId, reference])\n  @@index([businessId, status, riskLevel])\n  @@index([businessId, contactId])\n  @@index([businessId, assignmentId, status])\n  @@map(\"case_files\")\n}\n\nmodel CasePolicy {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  caseId     String   @map(\"case_id\")\n  case       CaseFile @relation(fields: [caseId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  policyNumber String  @map(\"policy_number\")\n  provider     String?                       // carrier / donor / awarding body\n  productName  String? @map(\"product_name\")\n\n  premiumAmount Decimal? @map(\"premium_amount\") @db.Decimal(14, 2)\n  currency      String   @default(\"TTD\")\n  sumInsured    Decimal? @map(\"sum_insured\") @db.Decimal(16, 2)\n\n  effectiveDate     DateTime? @map(\"effective_date\")\n  expiryDate        DateTime? @map(\"expiry_date\")\n  renewalDate       DateTime? @map(\"renewal_date\")\n  renewalNoticeDays Int?      @map(\"renewal_notice_days\")\n  autoRenew         Boolean   @default(false) @map(\"auto_renew\")\n  renewalRaisedAt   DateTime? @map(\"renewal_raised_at\")   // worker cursor, in the row\n\n  /// ACTIVE | PENDING_RENEWAL | RENEWED | LAPSED | CANCELLED\n  status String @default(\"ACTIVE\")\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@unique([businessId, policyNumber])\n  @@index([businessId, renewalDate, status])   // the renewal sweep's index\n  @@index([businessId, caseId])\n  @@map(\"case_policies\")\n}\n\nmodel CaseRequirement {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  caseId     String   @map(\"case_id\")\n  case       CaseFile @relation(fields: [caseId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  label      String                              // \"Proof of address\", \"Q3 donor report\"\n  kind       String   @default(\"DOCUMENT\")       // DOCUMENT | ACTION | PAYMENT | APPROVAL\n  dueAt      DateTime? @map(\"due_at\")\n  /// PENDING | SUBMITTED | ACCEPTED | REJECTED | WAIVED\n  status     String   @default(\"PENDING\")\n  /// Satisfied by an existing artefact rather than a new upload table.\n  evidenceId String?  @map(\"evidence_id\")\n  documentInstanceId String? @map(\"document_instance_id\")\n  satisfiedAt DateTime? @map(\"satisfied_at\")\n  raisedAt    DateTime? @map(\"raised_at\")        // worker cursor\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId, status, dueAt])\n  @@index([businessId, caseId])\n  @@map(\"case_requirements\")\n}\n\nmodel CaseEvent {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  caseId     String   @map(\"case_id\")\n  case       CaseFile @relation(fields: [caseId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  kind       String                              // NOTE | STATUS_CHANGE | CONTACT | DECISION | PAYMENT\n  summary    String\n  detail     String?  @db.Text\n  actorType  String   @default(\"USER\") @map(\"actor_type\")  // USER | KEY | SYSTEM\n  actorId    String?  @map(\"actor_id\")\n  occurredAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"occurred_at\")\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId, caseId, occurredAt])\n  @@map(\"case_events\")\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 The obligations `casework` emits\n\n| `actionType` | When | `executionTool` |\n|---|---|---|\n| `RENEWAL_DUE` | `renewalDate - renewalNoticeDays <= today`, `status = 'ACTIVE'`, `renewalRaisedAt IS NULL` | `casework_draft_renewal_notice` (T2, draft only) |\n| `POLICY_LAPSED` | `expiryDate < today`, `status ∉ (RENEWED, CANCELLED)` | — (human, T3 territory) |\n| `REQUIREMENT_OVERDUE` | `dueAt < today`, `status = 'PENDING'` | `casework_request_requirement` |\n| `CASE_STALLED` | `status = 'AWAITING_CLIENT'` and no `CaseEvent` for > `rules['casework.escalateAtDays']` | `casework_add_case_event` |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Module C — `roster` (shift/roster: the security firm)\n\n`apps/server/src/modules/roster/`\n\n### 5.1 Boundary\n\n**Owns:** `RosterPosition`, `RosterPattern`, `Shift`, `ShiftAttendance`.\n**Listens to:** nothing (deliberately — a roster's inputs are its own patterns and its own clock).\n**Emits:** obligations, plus `roster.shift.assigned|unfilled|missed|completed`.\n**Imports:** `[WorkProfileModule]`. **Imported by:** nothing.\n\n**Refusal carried forward:** this module produces **hours, not pay**. `capability map` §3.4 refuses T&T statutory payroll (PAYE, NIS, Health Surcharge) as a compliance product with an annual maintenance obligation. `ShiftAttendance` computes payable minutes and stops. Nothing here posts to the ledger and nothing here computes a deduction.\n\n### 5.2 Data\n\n```prisma\nmodel RosterPosition {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  name       String                             // \"Gate 2, night\"\n  siteId     String?  @map(\"site_id\")\n  site       ServiceSite? @relation(fields: [siteId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n  orgUnitId  String?  @map(\"org_unit_id\")\n  orgUnit    OrgUnit? @relation(fields: [orgUnitId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n\n  headcount        Int      @default(1)\n  requiredSkillIds String[] @default([]) @map(\"required_skill_ids\")\n  isActive         Boolean  @default(true) @map(\"is_active\")\n\n  patterns RosterPattern[]\n  shifts   Shift[]\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId, isActive])\n  @@index([businessId, siteId])\n  @@map(\"roster_positions\")\n}\n\nmodel RosterPattern {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  positionId String   @map(\"position_id\")\n  position   RosterPosition @relation(fields: [positionId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  /// An RFC-5545 RRULE string, parsed by rrule (BSD-3). Not hand-rolled.\n  rrule          String\n  timezone       String   @default(\"America/Port_of_Spain\")\n  startTimeLocal String   @map(\"start_time_local\")   // \"18:00\"\n  durationMinutes Int     @map(\"duration_minutes\")\n  effectiveFrom  DateTime @map(\"effective_from\")\n  effectiveTo    DateTime? @map(\"effective_to\")\n  /// The last instant materialised. The horizon cursor lives HERE, not in the\n  /// worker, so a restart or a second replica resumes at the same place.\n  materialisedThrough DateTime? @map(\"materialised_through\")\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId, positionId])\n  @@index([businessId, materialisedThrough])\n  @@map(\"roster_patterns\")\n}\n\nmodel Shift {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  positionId String   @map(\"position_id\")\n  position   RosterPosition @relation(fields: [positionId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  patternId  String?  @map(\"pattern_id\")\n  pattern    RosterPattern? @relation(fields: [patternId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n\n  startsAt DateTime @map(\"starts_at\")\n  endsAt   DateTime @map(\"ends_at\")\n\n  assignmentId String? @map(\"assignment_id\")\n  assignment   OrgAssignment? @relation(fields: [assignmentId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n\n  /// OPEN | ASSIGNED | CONFIRMED | IN_PROGRESS | COMPLETED | MISSED | CANCELLED\n  status String @default(\"OPEN\")\n\n  unfilledRaisedAt DateTime? @map(\"unfilled_raised_at\")   // cursors in the row\n  noShowRaisedAt   DateTime? @map(\"no_show_raised_at\")\n  notes            String?\n\n  attendance ShiftAttendance?\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  /// Materialisation is idempotent BY CONSTRAINT, not by a pre-read. The\n  /// generator runs daily from N replicas; this is what makes that safe.\n  @@unique([businessId, positionId, startsAt])\n  @@index([businessId, status, startsAt])\n  @@index([businessId, assignmentId, startsAt])\n  @@map(\"shifts\")\n}\n\nmodel ShiftAttendance {\n  id         String   @id @default(cuid())\n  businessId String   @map(\"business_id\")\n  business   Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n  shiftId    String   @unique @map(\"shift_id\")\n  shift      Shift    @relation(fields: [shiftId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)\n\n  clockInAt   DateTime? @map(\"clock_in_at\")\n  clockOutAt  DateTime? @map(\"clock_out_at\")\n  method      String    @default(\"APP\")        // APP | WHATSAPP | SUPERVISOR | IMPORT\n  clockInLatLng  Json?  @map(\"clock_in_lat_lng\")\n  clockOutLatLng Json?  @map(\"clock_out_lat_lng\")\n  lateMinutes    Int?   @map(\"late_minutes\")\n  payableMinutes Int?   @map(\"payable_minutes\")\n  verifiedBy     String? @map(\"verified_by\")\n  exception      String?                        // LATE | EARLY_LEAVE | NO_SHOW | RELIEVED\n  notes          String?\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map(\"created_at\")\n  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map(\"updated_at\")\n\n  @@index([businessId, clockInAt])\n  @@map(\"shift_attendances\")\n}\n```\n\n### 5.3 The obligations `roster` emits\n\n| `actionType` | When | `executionTool` |\n|---|---|---|\n| `SHIFT_UNFILLED` | `status = 'OPEN'` and `startsAt < now + rules['roster.unfilledAlertHoursBefore']` | `roster_assign_shift` |\n| `SHIFT_NO_SHOW` | `status = 'ASSIGNED'\\|'CONFIRMED'`, `startsAt + lateGraceMinutes < now`, no `clockInAt` | `roster_assign_shift` (relief) |\n| `SHIFT_NOT_CLOSED` | `endsAt < now - 2h`, `clockOutAt IS NULL` | `roster_record_attendance` |\n| `PATTERN_EXHAUSTED` | `materialisedThrough < now + horizon` and generation failed | — (human) |\n\n---\n\n## 6. The three layers, per module\n\n### 6.1 MANUAL — the screen\n\n| Route | Files | Nav | Must import (for the parity gate) |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `/app/dispatch` | `apps/web/src/app/app/dispatch/page.tsx` + `components/` | Operate → **Work** section, `nav-config.ts:231-248`, `workShape: 'dispatch'` | `createWorkOrder, assignWorkOrder, markWorkOrderComplete, cancelWorkOrder, recordVisitArrival` from `@/lib/api/dispatch` |\n| `/app/dispatch/[workOrderId]` | detail | `nav-reachability.spec.ts` → `'detail-route-of:/app/dispatch'` | — |\n| `/app/dispatch/units` | site + unit register | subtree-reachable | `createServicedUnit, updateServicedUnit` |\n| `/app/casework` | list + open/close dialogs | Operate → **Compliance**, `workShape: 'case'` | `createCaseFile, updateCasePolicy, resolveCaseRequirement, recordCaseEvent` from `@/lib/api/casework` |\n| `/app/casework/[caseId]` | detail | `'detail-route-of:/app/casework'` | — |\n| `/app/roster` | week grid + assign/publish | Operate → **Schedule**, `workShape: 'shift'` | `createShift, assignShift, recordShiftAttendance, generateShiftsFromPattern, cancelShift` from `@/lib/api/roster` |\n| `/app/settings/work-profile` | pack picker, vocabulary editor, rule editor | subtree of `/app/settings` | `updateWorkProfile` |\n\n**Two mechanical constraints an engineer will otherwise trip over:**\n\n1. **Mutation names must match the gate's verb list.** `tool-route-audit.ts:469-470` matches `^(create|update|delete|archive|publish|approve|send|cancel|assign|mark|resolve|submit|verify|remove|revoke|restore|import|generate|merge|take|record|apply|execute|refund|void|reconcile|fulfill|issue|acknowledge)[A-Z]`. **`close` is not on that list** — so `closeWorkOrder` and `closeCase` would read as read-only screens. Use `markWorkOrderComplete` and `resolveCaseFile`.\n2. **The mutation must be imported by the page or something ≤3 hops from it, and not through `lib/`.** `canWrite` (`:531-560`) recurses only through UI and stops dead at `lib/` — walking into the api layer is what made an earlier version of the gate unfailable (`:551-558`). So the *list* page must import the create/assign functions used by its dialogs. If the mutations live only on the detail route, the tools will need `MANUAL_PARITY_EXEMPTIONS` entries (`:267`) the way the seven `procurement_*` tools did. **Design the list pages so no exemption is needed.**\n\n`nav-reachability.spec.ts` `ORPHANED_BY_DESIGN` gains exactly three `detail-route-of:` entries and **no `todo-connect` entries.** That list is the backlog and it should only shrink.\n\n### 6.2 SMART — deterministic, no LLM\n\nThe BullMQ workers of §1.4 *are* the smart layer. Each is a pure function of (rows, `WorkRules`, clock) → obligations. No model call, no prompt, no `outcome: 'success'` written before the work.\n\n**One rule, stated because the SMART layer's central defect is exactly this:** a sweep that raises no obligation logs `raised: 0` and that is a real result; a sweep that *fails* throws and BullMQ marks the job failed. There is no third path where nothing happened and the log says it worked. `AutomationExecutorService.executePlaybooks` writing `outcome: 'success'` after a no-op loop is the pattern being refused.\n\nThese sweeps are **not** registered in the `/app/automations` trigger/action registry until that registry is unified (Wave 0 #3). Adding to a list that advertises 25 actions against 6 implementations makes the lie bigger.\n\n### 6.3 AI — the KEY tools\n\nAdded to `FLOW_TOOLS` in `apps/server/src/modules/ai/flow-tool-registry.ts`, and to `CORTEX_TOOL_BRIDGE` at `:4919` so `flow-orchestrator.service.ts` needs no `case` clause and no new import. `flow-tool-honesty.spec.ts:56-61` exempts bridged names from the case-clause rule and `cortex-tool-bridge.spec.ts` proves each one actually dispatches — both continue to hold.\n\n**Dispatch (12):**\n\n| Tool | family | riskTier | manualEquivalentRoute |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `dispatch_list_work_orders` | read | 1 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_get_work_order` | read | 1 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_list_sites` | read | 1 | `/app/dispatch/units` |\n| `dispatch_unit_service_history` | read | 1 | `/app/dispatch/units` |\n| `dispatch_technician_workload` | read | 1 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_create_work_order` | crud | 2 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_update_work_order` | crud | 2 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_assign_work_order` | organize | 2 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_record_visit` | crud | 2 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_complete_work_order` | execute | 2 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_invoice_work_order` | execute | 3 | `/app/dispatch` |\n| `dispatch_cancel_work_order` | execute | 3 | `/app/dispatch` |\n\n**Casework (10):** `casework_list_cases` (read/1), `casework_get_case` (read/1), `casework_list_renewals_due` (read/1), `casework_open_case` (crud/2), `casework_update_case` (crud/2), `casework_add_case_event` (crud/2), `casework_create_requirement` (crud/2), `casework_request_requirement` (execute/**3** — leaves the building), `casework_draft_renewal_notice` (draft/2), `casework_resolve_case` (execute/3). All → `/app/casework`.\n\n**Roster (9):** `roster_list_shifts` (read/1), `roster_list_unfilled` (read/1), `roster_coverage_summary` (read/1), `roster_create_shift` (crud/2), `roster_assign_shift` (organize/2), `roster_generate_shifts` (execute/2), `roster_record_attendance` (crud/2), `roster_cancel_shift` (execute/3), `roster_publish_roster` (execute/**3** — notifies guards). All → `/app/roster`.\n\n**Four registry facts, each checked against the code:**\n\n1. **Domain-parity passes without an exemption.** `tool-route-audit.ts:117-121` computes `domain = name.split('_')[0]` and tests `route.toLowerCase().replace(/[/-]/g,'').includes(domain)`. `\"appdispatch\".includes(\"dispatch\")` ✓, `\"appcasework\".includes(\"casework\")` ✓, `\"approster\".includes(\"roster\")` ✓. This is why the route is `/app/casework` and not `/app/cases` — `\"appcases\"` does not contain `\"casework\"`, and the fix is the route, not a `CROSS_DOMAIN_ROUTES` entry.\n2. **`capability-contract.service.ts:36-79` `OWNER_PREFIXES` gains three rows** — `['dispatch_', 'work']`, `['roster_', 'work']`, `['casework_', 'knowledge']` — otherwise all 31 fall through `ownerFor` to `'platform'`. The permission string is `${owner}.${family}.${name}` and `capability-contract.service.spec.ts:29` asserts its prefix.\n3. **Tier 3 must set `requiresApproval`** — derived at `capability-contract.service.ts:117` and asserted at `.spec.ts:35-37`.\n4. **Role allowlists** (`role-engine.service.ts`): `operations` (`:255`) gains `'dispatch_*'` and `'roster_*'`; `support` (`:236`) gains `'casework_*'`; `finance` (`:215`) gains `'casework_list_renewals_due'`, `'dispatch_invoice_work_order'`. `role-tool-reachability.spec.ts` will fail if a pattern matches zero tools — the defect that made `marketing`'s `'seo_*'` match nothing.\n\nTotal registry after this work: **245 + 31 = 276 tools.**\n\n### 6.4 Access control\n\n`RequireModuleScope('operations', 'write')` on every write handler in all three controllers, `AuthGuard, BusinessGuard` on all. **No new module key.** `module-scope.guard.ts:22-38` `DEFAULT_SCOPES` and `identity.service.ts:411` `PERMISSION_MODULES` are already out of sync — an invited ADMIN resolves `none` on all 58 `operations` handlers because the short map written at `identity.service.ts:506` has no `operations` key. Adding `dispatch`, `casework` and `roster` as new keys would create three more unreachable scopes on day one. **These modules ship under `operations`, and the reconciliation stays Wave 0 item #5.**\n\n---\n\n## 7. Tenant isolation and the migration\n\n### 7.1 The fourteen names\n\nAdd to `BUSINESS_ID_MODELS`, `packages/db/src/client.ts:81`, as a new commented block after the finance cluster:\n\n```ts\n  // ── Work shapes: dispatch, casework, roster (added 2026-08-__) ────────────\n  // Every one carries a businessId column and a cascading Business relation.\n  // A NEW MODEL IS UNSCOPED BY DEFAULT and tenant-model-list.spec.ts does not\n  // catch an unlisted one — it checks listed→exists and listed→has-businessId,\n  // never exists→listed. The converse gate ships with this work; see\n  // work-shape-models-are-scoped.spec.ts.\n  //\n  // What this does NOT protect, restated because these modules are\n  // clock-driven: the BullMQ workers below run with no tenant context, so\n  // these entries do nothing there. Every worker query carries an explicit\n  // `where: { businessId }` and that is its only scope.\n  'ServiceSite', 'ServicedUnit', 'WorkOrder', 'WorkOrderLine', 'WorkOrderVisit',\n  'CaseFile', 'CasePolicy', 'CaseRequirement', 'CaseEvent',\n  'RosterPosition', 'RosterPattern', 'Shift', 'ShiftAttendance',\n  'WorkProfile',\n```\n\nFourteen new models against a 439-model schema → **453**.\n\n### 7.2 The write path\n\nThe extension hooks ten operations and **`create`, `createMany`, `upsert`, `aggregate` and `groupBy` are not among them** (`client.ts:238-242`). So a cross-tenant create cannot be stopped there. Every controller binds `businessId` from `@Param('businessId')`, never from the body. Every DTO is a **class with `class-validator` decorators**, never an inline-typed `@Body()` — `flow.controller.ts:26` shows what an inline type costs: the global ValidationPipe whitelist does not strip it, and a client supplied its own `jobRoleEnvelope`.\n\n### 7.3 The migration\n\n`packages/db/prisma/migrations/2026____000000_work_shapes_and_profile/migration.sql`, hand-written in the house style of `20260722004808_payroll_mvp/migration.sql`: `CREATE TABLE` per model with `TEXT`/`TIMESTAMP(3)`/`DECIMAL(p,s)`, then `CREATE INDEX`, then `ALTER TABLE … ADD CONSTRAINT … FOREIGN KEY … ON DELETE CASCADE`. Additive only — no `ALTER` on an existing table beyond the implicit back-relations, which are Prisma-side and produce no DDL. Safe against the `_prisma_migrations` history reconciled by hand on 2026-08-09 (`OPEN_GAPS`, \"Prisma 7\").\n\nSeed: one `INSERT INTO work_profiles (business_id, pack_key, source) SELECT id, 'solo_gig', 'INFERRED' FROM businesses ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;` — every existing business gets a resolvable profile, none gets a new shape enabled.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Tests, each with the mutation that must make it fail\n\nThe house rule from `OPEN_GAPS` \"The gap under the gaps\": every defect this quarter was found by *running* something. Every test below runs something, and every one names its negative control.\n\n### 8.1 `apps/server/src/core/prisma/work-shape-models-are-scoped.spec.ts` — the converse gate\n\nThe existing `tenant-model-list.spec.ts` checks listed→real and listed→has-businessId. It does not check **real→listed**, which is why a new model is unscoped by default. This adds it, for the models this work introduces and every future one:\n\n```\nevery model in schema.prisma that HAS a businessId column and is not on\nTENANT_EXEMPT must appear in BUSINESS_ID_MODELS.\n```\n\n`TENANT_EXEMPT` starts with `TaskAssignment` (no businessId — documented at `client.ts:77-80`), `Payment` and `MarketplaceOrder` (webhook-resolved by global provider key — `client.ts:137-152`), each carrying the existing reason verbatim.\n\n**Negative control:** the analyser is a pure function `analyse(schemaText, clientText)`. The spec calls it a second time with the schema text unchanged and `'WorkOrder',` deleted from the client text, and asserts `WorkOrder` is reported. If the detector ever stops working, that assertion fails and the green result above is not believed. *This is the whole reason the analyser takes text arguments rather than reading files itself.*\n\n### 8.2 `apps/server/test/obligation-command.integration.test.ts` — real Postgres\n\n`vitest.integration.config.ts` includes `test/**/*.integration.test.ts`; CI supplies `pgvector/pgvector:pg16` + `db:deploy`. Shape copied from `test/cortex-context-v2.integration.test.ts:35-56`, including the `setTenantContextProvider` stand-in for the AsyncLocalStorage that only exists on the HTTP path.\n\n1. Seed a business + a `WorkOrder` with `slaDueAt` in the past.\n2. Emit `work.obligation.raised` twice with identical keys → assert **exactly one** `command_items` row.\n3. **Negative control:** emit a third time with `actionType: 'WORK_ORDER_UNASSIGNED'` → assert **two** rows. Without this, a listener that silently did nothing would pass step 2. This is the \"assertions check referents, not shapes\" failure spelled out: step 2 alone cannot distinguish dedupe from no-op.\n4. Dismiss the row by hand, re-raise → assert `status` is still `DISMISSED`. A sweep that reopens what a human dismissed is a nag loop.\n5. Emit `work.obligation.settled` → assert `COMPLETED` with `completedAt` set.\n\n### 8.3 `apps/server/test/roster-clock.integration.test.ts` — the replica test\n\nThe one that proves the SCALABLE constraint held.\n\n1. Real BullMQ against the CI Redis. Start **two** `Worker` instances on `roster-clock` with a counting processor.\n2. Register the repeatable `roster:materialise` from **two** simulated module inits.\n3. Advance to two fire windows → assert the processor ran exactly twice, and `queue.getRepeatableJobs()` has length 1.\n4. **Negative control:** in the same file, `queue.add('x', {}, {})` twice with no `jobId` and assert the processor runs **twice more**. That proves the harness can observe a double-fire — without it, a broken worker that never runs at all passes step 3.\n5. Materialise a `RosterPattern` from both workers concurrently → assert `shifts` row count equals the RRULE's occurrence count, relying on `@@unique([businessId, positionId, startsAt])` and a `P2002` catch, not a pre-read.\n\n### 8.4 `apps/server/test/dispatch-sla-clock.integration.test.ts`\n\nReschedule a work order; assert the old delayed job id is gone from `getDelayed()` and the new `dispatch:sla:<id>:<newMs>` is present. **Negative control:** assert that *not* rescheduling leaves the original id — otherwise a `removeAll` bug passes.\n\n### 8.5 `apps/server/src/modules/business-genome/work-profile/work-profile.service.spec.ts`\n\n- `ngo_casework` resolves `client` → `beneficiary` / `beneficiaries`.\n- **Negative control, same test:** `solo_gig` resolves `client` → `client` / `clients`. A resolver that returned the key unchanged passes the second and fails the first; one that returned a hard-coded string passes the first and fails the second. Neither assertion alone is worth anything.\n- `enabledShapes` for `salon` excludes `'dispatch'`; a `ruleOverride` of `{ 'casework.renewalNoticeDays': 30 }` beats the pack's 45; an unknown rule key is rejected rather than merged.\n- Every pack in `WORK_PROFILE_PACKS` supplies all seven `WorkTermKey`s and only declared `WorkRules` keys.\n\n### 8.6 `apps/server/src/modules/ai/obligation-execution-tool.spec.ts`\n\nEvery `executionTool` string emitted anywhere in `modules/dispatch`, `modules/casework`, `modules/roster` must exist in `FLOW_TOOLS`. **Negative control:** the spec's own extractor is run against a fixture containing `executionTool: 'not_a_real_tool'` and must report it. This is the `CommandItem.executionTool` version of the `autoExecutable`-written-never-read defect, closed before it happens.\n\n### 8.7 Gates that must stay green with no new exemption\n\n`apps/web/src/lib/__tests__/tool-routes.spec.ts` (276/276, zero `MANUAL_PARITY_EXEMPTIONS` added), `nav-reachability.spec.ts` (three `detail-route-of:` entries, zero `todo-connect`), `nav-destinations.spec.ts`, `event-wiring.spec.ts` (`KNOWN_DEAD` unchanged at 10 — the three new listeners all have emitters in this same spec's scope), `capability-contract.service.spec.ts`, `role-tool-reachability.spec.ts`, `flow-tool-honesty.spec.ts`, `app-module-boots.integration.test.ts`.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Effort, and what to adopt\n\n| Item | Days | Notes |\n|---|---:|---|\n| `WorkProfileModule` + packs + controller + web context + settings screen | **6** | 10 packs at ~20 lines each is the bulk. |\n| Obligation contract + `ObligationListener` + §8.1/§8.2/§8.6 specs | **5** | The listener is 60 lines. The tests are the work. |\n| `dispatch` — 5 models, service, controller, queue, 12 tools, 3 screens | **14** | Largest: the visit/travel model and the unit history join. |\n| `casework` — 4 models, service, controller, queue, 10 tools, 2 screens | **10** | Renewal sweep also discharges Wave 0 #2. |\n| `roster` — 4 models, service, controller, queue+RRULE, 9 tools, week grid | **12** | The week grid is 4 of the 12. |\n| Migration, `BUSINESS_ID_MODELS`, nav, disclosure labels, role allowlists, `OWNER_PREFIXES` | **3** | Mechanical, and each has a gate that fails if missed. |\n| Integration tests §8.3–§8.5 against real Redis + Postgres | **4** | |\n| **Total** | **54 days** | ≈ 11 weeks, one engineer.
